Use before submission or before sharing a draft. ([djjay0131/agentic-research](https://github.com/djjay0131/agentic-research/blob/HEAD/research/agents/originality-agent.md) · ★1)
- **originality-checker**: Cek apakah SCRIPT.md transformative terhadap script referensi (overlap teks + premis + twist). Dipanggil di Step 3. ([fahniazz/claude-shorts-skills](https://github.com/fahniazz/claude-shorts-skills/blob/HEAD/skills/minecraft-story/agents/originality-checker.md) · ★1)
- **originality-checker**: Checks Fork's code and docs for originality risks before commits and phase sign-offs. Flags code that resembles other engines too closely (structure, names, constants, comments) and verifies docs/ORIGINALITY.md is complete and accurate. ([BOSSDOTCOM/fork](https://github.com/BOSSDOTCOM/fork/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/originality-checker.md))
- **originality-checker**: Independent check that a game design or finished game does not borrow protected names, characters, art or levels from existing games. Use with a DESIGN.md and any screenshots, before building and before publishing. ([gbouslov/gamestudio](https://github.com/gbouslov/gamestudio/blob/HEAD/gamestudio/agents/originality-checker.md) · 2 copies)
- **originality-checker**: The ORIGINALITY-SCAN gate — an independent scan of the chapter's distinctive prose spans for inadvertent verbatim / near-verbatim regurgitation of memorized or copyrighted text against the BROAD web (beyond the _ref/ corpus). Use at PIPELINE step 5b, in Phase 3 after Step 5 VERIFY and before Step 6 CLARITY. HARD gate. MUST be run on a DIFFERENT MODEL / PERSONA than the drafter — the independence is the point; a model cannot reliably detect its own regurgitation. ([Pratiyush/book-java-code-quality](https://github.com/Pratiyush/book-java-code-quality/blob/HEAD/.foundation/agents/originality-checker.md) · 2 copies)
- **originality-critic**: Detects generic AI-aesthetic convergence and templated output, and steers visual and structural choices toward intentional, product-fitting design. ([Suraj787/motif](https://github.com/Suraj787/motif/blob/HEAD/agents/originality-critic.md))
- **originality-reviewer**: 独創性軸の評価専門エージェント。コードの洗練度・工夫・要件を超えた価値を定量評価する。ボイラープレート率・共通化・宣言的実装の観点から分析する ([ac2393921/long-running-harness](https://github.com/ac2393921/long-running-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/originality-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **originality-reviewer**: 原创性评审员：独立于设计师，比对避让清单做原创性审查，做 16px/单色/深底生存测试，给出商标风险提示（不承诺结果）。适用于 logo-critique 命令。 ([lhg-plugs/lhg-logo](https://github.com/lhg-plugs/lhg-logo/blob/HEAD/agents/originality-reviewer.md))
- **Orion**: Email marketing strategy, automation sequences, segmentation, copywriting, and performance analysis. Use for any email campaign, newsletter, or marketing automation tasks. ([djbelieny/nova](https://github.com/djbelieny/nova/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/orion.md) · ★5)
- **ORION**: Elite Coder agent for all programming, development, and architecture tasks. Handles writing code, refactoring, implementing features, and full-stack development across all languages. ([azerxafro/AZERCLAW](https://github.com/azerxafro/AZERCLAW/blob/HEAD/agents/orion.md))
- **orion**: Functional UI hunter. Persists ORI candidates for behavior, forms, and client state; presentation belongs to Lynceus, accessibility to Antigone, and validation to Minos. ([holi87/holak-teams](https://github.com/holi87/holak-teams/blob/HEAD/argus/claude/agents/orion.md))
- **Orion**: Virtual cinematographer who directs VEO for cinema-quality clips. Orion is the master of camera movement, lighting, and scene composition within the AI generation pipeline. He treats VEO not as a black box but as a virtual camera rig — one with specific capabilities, known behaviors, and techniques for extracting the best possible footage. He understands that generating video is not the same as re ([jlouage/COOKIE---AI-Video-Production-Framework](https://github.com/jlouage/COOKIE---AI-Video-Production-Framework/blob/HEAD/_cookie/production/agents/cinematographer.md))
- **orion**: Use for deep-dive analysis of support/incident ticket dumps. Orion is an SRE & observability intelligence agent that runs a 3-question onboarding (client, time scope, ticket data), analyzes the dump through 7 observability lenses (volume/trend, severity, category, MTTR & closure-time, recurrence/toil, root cause, maturity gaps), prints a structured Pre-Dashboard Analysis Brief, then generates a single self-contained interactive HTML dashboard (Chart.js via CDN). Invoke when the user wants to turn raw tickets (CSV/JSON/table/text) into an executive-ready observability report or dashboard. ([lloydisaac1982/orion-observability](https://github.com/lloydisaac1982/orion-observability/blob/HEAD/orion/agents/orion.md))
- **orion**: The Hunter — Interactive debugging subagent. Collects problem descriptions, investigates root causes, proposes solutions, and iterates with the user until the quarry is caught. ([NICxKMS/auto-coursera](https://github.com/NICxKMS/auto-coursera/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/orion.agent.md))
- **orion**: The Hunter -- Interactive debugging specialist. Collects problem descriptions, investigates root causes, proposes solutions, and iterates with the user until the quarry is caught. ([NICxKMS/auto-coursera](https://github.com/NICxKMS/auto-coursera/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/orion.md))
- **orion**: Reads for the path nothing exercises. Dispatch him at code and at proposals - to find the case with no test, the invariant that passes for the wrong reason, the branch that only runs on the other platform. He is also the one to give a design you already like, because he volunteers the argument against his own proposal. ([Weibye/plexify](https://github.com/Weibye/plexify/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/orion.md))
- **orion-repo-agent**: General-purpose coding agent for Orion-Sapienform with this repo's own conventions baked into its system prompt. Use instead of general-purpose for any dispatched task that touches this repo's code, docs, tests, or git history -- implementation, investigation, review-angle work, verification -- so the subagent doesn't need the orchestrator to re-paste worktree/env/graphify instructions by hand every time. Not needed for pure read-only lookups scoped to a single named file. ([junebug-junie/Orion-Sapienform](https://github.com/junebug-junie/Orion-Sapienform/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/orion-repo-agent.md) · ★2)
- **orion-repo-agent**: Sub-agent for one multi-step piece of work in Orion's own repo checkout (code, tests, docs, git history) that needs Bash and may edit files -- e.g. trace how a feature works end to end, run tests, or make and commit a change on a branch. Give it a self-contained brief; it returns a short summary with evidence. For read-only searches across many files, use Explore instead. ([junebug-junie/Orion-Sapienform](https://github.com/junebug-junie/Orion-Sapienform/blob/HEAD/orion/fcc/agents/orion-repo-agent.md) · ★2)
- **orionite**: Vault mechanic — runs an Orion vault's mechanical passes end to end: orion-cascade and orion-lint, scans included, EXCEPT the People armed-tier sweep (that's orionite-people). Use for any vault operation that is glob/grep/parse/compare rather than judgment. Returns only the skill's Output format block, never file contents or grep dumps. Do NOT use for orion-ingest, orion-review or orion-daily — those classify ambiguous content and ask the owner questions. ([kyleisuncool/orion-boilerplate](https://github.com/kyleisuncool/orion-boilerplate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/orionite.md))
- **orionite-people**: People-sweep calculator — computes an Orion vault's People armed-tier (tier: relationship) follow-up sweep for orion-cascade and orion-lint. Cross-references a handful of near-identical person files with zero tolerance for tier substitution. Returns only the computed Overdue/Due-soon/Defects lines, never file contents or reasoning. ([kyleisuncool/orion-boilerplate](https://github.com/kyleisuncool/orion-boilerplate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/orionite-people.md))

- **orive**: Use as the default entry point and host. Greets the user, works out what they need, names the specialist it is bringing in, asks only the few questions that matter, and runs the connection wizard when a task needs a tool that is not connected yet. Triggers on orive, studio, start, hi, hello, open it, and any unclear or first-time request. ([bishrant-personal/orive-creative-studio](https://github.com/bishrant-personal/orive-creative-studio/blob/HEAD/agents/orive.md))
- **ork-check**: Conduz a fase CHECK: reexecuta a verificacao contra a baseline, percorre os cinco eixos e consolida um veredito unico. Nao corrige o que encontra. ([orkastery/orkastery](https://github.com/orkastery/orkastery/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/agents/ork-check.md) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **ork-check**: Conducts the CHECK phase: re-runs the verification against the baseline, goes through the five axes and consolidates a single verdict. Does not fix what it finds. ([orkastery/orkastery](https://github.com/orkastery/orkastery/blob/HEAD/core/assets/adapters-en/claude-code/agents/ork-check.md) · ★6)
- **ork-go**: Conducts the GO phase inside the thread's worktree: one atomic commit per task, baseline recorded before starting. ([orkastery/orkastery](https://github.com/orkastery/orkastery/blob/HEAD/core/assets/adapters-en/claude-code/agents/ork-go.md) · ★6)
- **ork-go**: Conduz a fase GO dentro da worktree da thread: um commit atomico por tarefa, baseline gravada antes de comecar. ([orkastery/orkastery](https://github.com/orkastery/orkastery/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/agents/ork-go.md) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **ork-goal**: Conduz a fase GOAL de uma thread do Orkastery: exploracao do repositorio real, premissas explicitas e criterios que viram claims verificaveis. Nao implementa. ([orkastery/orkastery](https://github.com/orkastery/orkastery/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/agents/ork-goal.md) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **ork-goal**: Conducts the GOAL phase of an Orkastery thread: exploration of the real repository, explicit assumptions and criteria that become verifiable claims. Does not implement. ([orkastery/orkastery](https://github.com/orkastery/orkastery/blob/HEAD/core/assets/adapters-en/claude-code/agents/ork-goal.md) · ★6)
- **ork-master**: Conducts the MASTER phase: typed POSTMORTEM, MASTER log in the frozen contract and collection of the human score. Never makes up a score. ([orkastery/orkastery](https://github.com/orkastery/orkastery/blob/HEAD/core/assets/adapters-en/claude-code/agents/ork-master.md) · ★6)
- **ork-master**: Conduz a fase MASTER: POSTMORTEM tipado, MASTER log no contrato congelado e coleta do score humano. Nunca inventa nota. ([orkastery/orkastery](https://github.com/orkastery/orkastery/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/agents/ork-master.md) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **ork-plan**: Conduz a fase PLAN: tarefas fatiadas com touch_paths e verify por tarefa, decisoes com domicilio unico. Nao implementa. ([orkastery/orkastery](https://github.com/orkastery/orkastery/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/agents/ork-plan.md) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **ork-plan**: Conducts the PLAN phase: sliced tasks with touch_paths and a verify per task, decisions with a single home. Does not implement. ([orkastery/orkastery](https://github.com/orkastery/orkastery/blob/HEAD/core/assets/adapters-en/claude-code/agents/ork-plan.md) · ★6)
- **ork-ship**: Conduz a fase SHIP pelo `ork ship`: merge serializado por lease e push provado por comando. ([orkastery/orkastery](https://github.com/orkastery/orkastery/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/agents/ork-ship.md) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **ork-ship**: Conducts the SHIP phase through `ork ship`: merge serialized by lease and push proven by command. ([orkastery/orkastery](https://github.com/orkastery/orkastery/blob/HEAD/core/assets/adapters-en/claude-code/agents/ork-ship.md) · ★6)
- **orkas-ai-orkas**: Orkas는 MIT 라이선스로 공개된 local-first 멀티에이전트 데스크톱 앱이다. 사용자는 Commander라는 조율 에이전트 하나와 대화하고, Commander가 목표를 할 일 목록으로 나눈 뒤 직접 처리하거나 내장 전문 에이전트 16개와 사용자가 이미 설치한 CLI 에이전트에 일을 나눠 맡긴다. 결과물은 답변 한 줄이 아니라 보고서, 슬라이드 덱, 영상, 코드 변경 같은 완성된 파일이다. - **orquestrador**: Agente primário do ExpxBlog. É com ele que o usuário se comunica. Recebe qualquer tarefa, entende o domínio envolvido, decompõe em subtarefas e delega para os agentes especializados corretos. Quando o pedido cai num domínio que nenhum agente existente cobre, CONTRATA um novo agente (cria o agente, suas skills e rules) — sempre avisando o usuário e pedindo OK antes de criar. Nunca implementa código diretamente — seu papel é coordenar, revisar os resultados e entregar um resumo coeso ao usuário. Sempre termina chamando o agent reviewer antes de considerar a tarefa concluída. - **ose-researcher**: You are an Open Source Europe (OSE) documentation researcher. Your job is to query the `ose-knowledge` MCP server (tool `search_docs`) and return concise, verified, cited findings. ([opensourceeurope/ose-knowledge-mcp](https://github.com/opensourceeurope/ose-knowledge-mcp/blob/HEAD/plugins/ose-knowledge/agents/ose-researcher.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **osf-analyze**: Codebase structural analysis using GitNexus knowledge graph + codebase-retrieval. Traces dependencies, blast radius, call chains, and impact. ([nullmastermind/godot-turnbase](https://github.com/nullmastermind/godot-turnbase/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/osf-analyze.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **osf-apply**: Implement tasks from OpenSpec change or conversation plan. Writes code, completes tasks, modifies files. ([nullmastermind/godot-turnbase](https://github.com/nullmastermind/godot-turnbase/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/osf-apply.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **osf-apply-abort**: Abort implementation for an approved OpenSpec change when the worker must stop. Rolls back the working branch, preserves investigation if useful, checks out main, and returns an extensive human-facing debrief. Does not edit change artifacts. Use when osf-apply-start delegates with blocker context or /osf-apply-abort is invoked. ([jcwilk/cofracture](https://github.com/jcwilk/cofracture/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/osf-apply-abort.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **osf-apply-finish**: Verify a working branch against its approved OpenSpec change, archive the change on that branch, commit, and push the working branch. Use proactively when osf-apply-start delegates finish or implementation is otherwise complete on the working branch. ([jcwilk/cofracture](https://github.com/jcwilk/cofracture/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/osf-apply-finish.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **osf-apply-finish**: Verify a working branch against its approved OpenSpec change, archive the change on that branch, merge into main, and push. Use proactively when osf-apply-start delegates finish or implementation is otherwise complete on the working branch. ([jcwilk/akoya_unikernel](https://github.com/jcwilk/akoya_unikernel/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/osf-apply-finish.md) · 2 copies)
- **osf-apply-start**: Implement one approved OpenSpec change end-to-end on the current branch, then delegate finish or abort. Owns the full task work-queue and routing to osf-apply-finish or osf-apply-abort. Use proactively when /osf-apply-changes spawns one apply unit. ([jcwilk/cofracture](https://github.com/jcwilk/cofracture/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/osf-apply-start.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **osf-archive**: Archive a completed change. Finalizes and moves change to archive directory. ([nullmastermind/godot-turnbase](https://github.com/nullmastermind/godot-turnbase/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/osf-archive.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
